DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [167] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentHPOBench，一个包含30个可执行机器学习任务、覆盖七个研究类别的序列式基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在实验环境中进行超参数优化（HPO）的能力。评测12种主流智能体和传统HPO基线后发现，现有智能体虽展现出一定的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代改进、复杂日志诊断和稳定向参考性能收敛方面仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要聚焦于静态代码生成、论文复现或最终答案正确性，无法直接评估智能体是否能够解读实验证据并据此指导后续超参数决策。随着LLM从代码补全工具演变为自主科学智能体，亟需一个能够评测其序贯实验能力的基准。

Method: 构建AgentHPOBench序列式基准测试，包含30个可执行机器学习任务，覆盖七个研究类别。每个任务以验证过的基线运行为起点，智能体随后执行多次序贯干预；每一步中，智能体观察累积的配置、指标和日志后再提出下一个有效配置。在统一协议下评估12种主流智能体及传统HPO基线。

Result: 现有智能体在跨领域上展现出可测量的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代精化、复杂日志诊断以及稳定逼近参考表现方面仍存在明确的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在自主实验优化方面已具备一定基础能力，但距离真正可靠的科学发现工具仍有显著差距，尤其是在长程迭代推理和复杂信号解析方面需要进一步改进。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [168] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FDD-ON，一个模块化、可扩展的本体，用于形式化表示VAV HVAC系统的组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性，通过标准化的术语和因果关系库为FDD提供机器可解释的语义基础，促进异构FDD应用的互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDD技术在提高HVAC系统可靠性和能效方面至关重要，但FDD领域的部署面临数据可解释性和互操作性不足的问题，导致信息孤岛现象，阻碍了FDD及数字孪生、AI驱动的维护决策等下游应用的实施。

Method: 论文提出FDD-ON本体框架：(1) 采用模块化和可扩展的设计，形式化表示VAV HVAC系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及属性；(2) 整合HVAC系统FDD语义，提供受控词汇表；(3) 构建全面的故障、症状和影响库；(4) 建立因果-故障-症状-影响之间的显式关系。

Result: FDD-ON使用公开可用的VAV HVAC系统数据集进行评估，并通过FDD开发应用进行验证。结果表明FDD-ON为跨多种应用场景推进可扩展、透明和可互操作的FDD解决方案提供了基础语义框架。

Conclusion: FDD-ON可作为机器可解释的诊断知识查询基础，支持异构FDD输出的映射以及可互操作FDD应用的开发，为数字孪生和AI驱动的HVAC维护提供了语义支撑。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [169] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExtractBench，一个面向企业级模式引导提取任务的基准测试，首次同时评估值准确率、记录完整性、来源可追溯性和成本四个维度。基准包含370篇企业文档（4,869页）、8个业务领域、67种文档类型，通过可扩展的标注流水线生成真值。实验发现商业VLM在短文档上表现良好但在长文档上会截断记录列表，编码代理准确率更高但成本显著更高。LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在所有三个指标上排名第一，其准确率与编码代理相当但成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流日益依赖代理进行模式引导提取（schema-guided extraction），即代理根据用户定义的模式从文档中提取结构化输出并附带源证据。然而现有基准缺少对提取任务多维度（值准确性、记录完整性、可追溯性、成本）的联合评估，因此需要一个新的综合基准来衡量这些关键属性。

Method: 构建了一个包含4,869页、370份企业文档、8个业务领域和67种文档类型的评估系统，通过可扩展的schema和真值标注流水线（结合独立系统一致性、合成列表已知值和人工验证表单）来生成ground truth。评估指标包括顺序不敏感的值F1（值准确率）、词级和页级F1（可追溯性），并记录测量成本。

Result: 商业VLM在短文档上表现良好但处理长文档时常截断记录列表；编码代理保持较高准确率但成本显著更高；LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在全部三个指标上排名第一，在取得与编码代理相当准确率的同时将成本降至其一小部分。

Conclusion: ExtractBench为模式引导提取提供了首个多维度综合基准，揭示了不同模型家族在准确率、可追溯性和成本之间的权衡，表明专用提取代理（如LlamaExtract Agentic Plus）可在成本和性能之间达到更优平衡。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [170] [Evidence-Ledger Adjudication for Claim-Evidence Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26512)
*Gengyu Chen,Yongjie Yu,Weiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"证据台账裁决"（evidence-ledger adjudication）工作流，将每个AI生成的声明与证据包配对、标注支持关系，并将无证据、矛盾或证据混合的声明退回作者。基于2,335行的盲测基准，该方法的裁决准确率达0.676、macro-F1为0.601，显著优于非智能体基线，表明其可作为AI辅助写作的可审计追踪层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体生成声明的速度快于作者核验引用或检索证据是否支持这些声明的速度，存在"声明-证据"一致性不可控的问题，亟需一种系统化的可追溯机制来保障写作的可信度。

Method: 提出证据台账裁决工作流：将每条声明与证据包配对并标注支持关系，将无证据、矛盾或混合证据的声明路由回作者。构建包含2,335行、来源覆盖AVeriTeC、CLIMATE-FEVER和SciFact三个数据集的盲测基准，隐藏黄金关系与来源证据标签，仅在评分时结合。

Result: 智能体证据台账条件下的关系准确率为0.676、macro-F1为0.601，优于最佳非智能体基线（准确率0.383、macro-F1 0.303）。在1,435条黄金标签为矛盾、缺证或混合证据的声明中成功路由1,270条，同时路由了900条支持声明中的295条。

Conclusion: 证据台账裁决能够将异构证据包转化为AI辅助写作的可审计追踪层，有效识别并回退问题声明，提升写作流程的可信度与可追溯性。

Abstract: AI agents can draft claims faster than authors can check whether the cited or retrieved evidence supports them. We study evidence-ledger adjudication: a claim-evidence traceability workflow that pairs each claim with an evidence packet, assigns a support relation, and routes unsupported, contradicted, or mixed-evidence claims back to the author. The empirical core is a 2,335-row blind benchmark built from independent external labels in AVeriTeC, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact. Gold relations and source evidence labels are hidden during prediction and joined only for scoring. On this benchmark, the agent evidence-ledger condition achieves 0.676 relation accuracy and 0.601 macro-F1, compared with 0.383 accuracy and 0.303 macro-F1 for the best non-agent baseline. It also routes 1270/1435 claims whose gold labels indicate contradiction, missing evidence, or mixed evidence, while routing 295/900 supported claims. These results show that evidence-ledger adjudication can turn heterogeneous evidence packets into an auditable traceability layer for AI-assisted writing.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Meshy T2: Fast Native Mesh Generation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28675)
*Jiale Xu,Rendong Liang,Yuhao Long,Siyuan Shen,Zangyueyang Xian,Zeyi Xu,Yuanming Hu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Meshy T2是一个基于flow matching的快速原生网格生成框架，通过顶点集网格VAE将网格编码为每个顶点一个连续潜变量token，并用两级粗到细的flow-matching模型（体素flow和网格flow）实现图像到网格的交互式生成，中位数6秒完成端到端生成，比自回归基线快一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流方法将网格序列化为token序列并自回归解码，推理速度慢且对错误累积敏感，无法满足交互式资产生成的实际需求。亟需一种既能保持高精度几何和艺术家风格拓扑，又能快速生成的网格生成框架。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 顶点集网格VAE：将网格编码为每个顶点一个连续潜变量token，单遍解码顶点坐标、边连接性和面绕序，无需顶点量化或焊接；(2) 两级粗到细级联flow-matching：图像条件体素flow先生成粗占用脚手架，网格flow再基于图像、脚手架和顶点预算为每个顶点填充潜变量token；(3) 所请求的顶点预算实现有效的面数控制；(4) 多部件资产生成能力直接来源于生成的连接性。

Result: Meshy T2在几何保真度上达到最先进水平，端到端图像到网格生成中位时间6秒，比自回归基线快一个数量级以上，同时支持面数控制和多部件资产生成。

Conclusion: Meshy T2通过并行flow-based合成替代自回归解码，成功解决了网格生成中推理速度慢和错误累积的问题，在保持高精度几何与艺术家拓扑的同时实现了交互式生成速度，为实时3D资产生成提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Polygonal meshes are the standard surface representation of modern 3D pipelines, and generating high-quality meshes with artist-style topology is essential for film, gaming, and interactive 3D applications. Mainstream approaches serialize a mesh into a token sequence and decode it autoregressively, which is slow at inference and sensitive to error accumulation, making them impractical for interactive asset creation. We present Meshy T2, a fast native mesh generation framework built on flow matching. At its core is a vertex-set mesh VAE that encodes a mesh into one continuous latent token per vertex and decodes vertices, edge connectivity, and face winding order in a single pass, preserving high-precision geometry and artist-authored topology without vertex quantization or welding. Generation proceeds as a coarse-to-fine cascade of two flow-matching models: an image-conditioned voxel flow first sketches the overall shape as a coarse occupancy scaffold, and a mesh flow then populates the scaffold with per-vertex latent tokens, conditioned on the image, the scaffold, and a requested vertex budget. This design delivers three practical capabilities: interactive generation speed through parallel flow-based synthesis; effective face-count control through the requested vertex budget; and native support for multi-part assets, whose components emerge directly from the generated connectivity. In our experiments, Meshy T2 achieves state-of-the-art geometric fidelity and completes end-to-end image-to-mesh generation within a median of 6 seconds, over an order of magnitude faster than autoregressive baselines. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/meshy-dev/meshy-t2.

</details>


### [172] [Learning Manifolds in High-D Point Embedding for Anisotropic Surface Approximation from Unstructured Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28855)
*Hongbo Li,Haikuan Zhu,Xiaohu Guo,Wenping Wang,Jing Hua,Zichun Zhong*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出HD-PEA，一个高效且可扩展的基于学习的各向异性曲面逼近框架，直接在非结构化点云上操作，将各向异性优化集成到重建中，通过高维欧几里得点嵌入和切子空间估计，生成更紧凑、几何对齐的曲面表示，相比各向同性和自适应网格具有更高保真度、更少元素和更好的数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中各种应用产生的密集3D点云在实时处理时存在几何冗余，需要高效的曲面逼近方法将点云压缩为紧凑的曲面表示，但现有方法在保真度、元素数量和数值稳定性方面仍有不足。

Method: 1) 提出新颖的基于学习的高维欧几里得点嵌入方法，将输入点云映射到高维流形嵌入空间；2) 在推理阶段设计基于patch的元嵌入方案，无需重新训练即可处理大规模点云；3) 开发新的切子空间估计方法，用于高维嵌入流形的逼近和各向异性流形重建；4) 提出可扩展的深度学习框架并构建多种数据集。

Result: 在Thingi10K、AIM@SHAPE、Stanford 3D Scanning Repository和ScanNet等多个数据集上与最先进的曲面重建方法进行广泛评估，展示了方法在未见形状上的泛化能力和实用性。

Conclusion: HD-PEA通过将各向异性优化引入深度学习重建框架，实现了紧凑、几何对齐的高保真曲面表示，并在多个基准数据集上验证了其优越性、可扩展性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Dense 3D sensors in various real-world fields produce point clouds that are geometrically redundant for real-time processing. In this paper, we propose an efficient and scalable learning-based anisotropic surface approximation framework, HD-PEA, that operates directly on unstructured point clouds, integrating anisotropic optimization into reconstruction to produce compact, geometry-aligned surface representations with higher fidelity, fewer elements, and improved numerical stability compared to isotropic and adaptive meshes. Firstly, we develop a novel learning-based high-dimensional (high-d) Euclidean point embedding method to map the input point clouds into a high-d manifold embedding space. For handling large-scale point clouds without retraining and fine-tuning, a patch-based meta-embedding scheme is designed during the inference stage. Then, we develop a new tangent subspace estimation for the high-d embedding manifold approximation and anisotropic manifold reconstruction in high-d space. The main contribution of this work is to propose a scalable deep learning framework and a variety of datasets for constructing a high-d Euclidean point embedding space aimed to 3D anisotropic surface mesh approximation and Riemannian curvature tensor estimation from point clouds. We extensively evaluate our method against state-of-the-art surface reconstruction approaches using several datasets, such as Thingi10K dataset, AIM@SHAPE and Stanford 3D Scanning Repository, ScanNet dataset, and further demonstrate its generalization and usability on diverse unseen shapes and applications from these datasets.

</details>


### [173] [Interactive Generative Motion Editing via Scheduled Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29133)
*Dhruv Agrawal,Dominik Borer,Luca Vögeli,Robert Sumner,Martin Guay,Jakob Buhmann*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"计划式补全"(scheduled inpainting)的推理级技术，通过利用生成模型实现交互式生成运动编辑，统一了运动合成与编辑两大范式，支持对已有动画进行细粒度时空控制下的保留与生成平衡，可用于运动延长、拼接和合成等多种编辑应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动编辑只能处理小幅修改，无法应对大型结构编辑，会产生视觉扭曲伪影；而近期大规模生成模型虽能通过空间约束生成新运动，却缺乏保留和交互式编辑已有运动的能力。两种范式之间存在明显的鸿沟。

Method: 提出scheduled inpainting方法，这是一种基于推理的简单但强大的技术，构建在支持直接操纵的生成模型之上。通过对原始运动保留与新内容生成之间的平衡进行细粒度的时空控制，实现交互式生成运动编辑。

Result: 该方法支持多种编辑应用，包括运动延长、拼接和不同片段的合成。通过与四个基线方法的比较、设计消融实验以及用户反馈，对方法进行了广泛验证。

Conclusion: 计划式补全是一种通用且有效的交互式生成运动编辑方法，能够在保持生成结果自然且符合所学运动分布的前提下，让艺术家交互式地精修已有动画，弥合了运动合成与编辑之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Motion editing is central to VFX and game development, where it is used extensively to modify and augment existing movements to conform to new environments or changes in artistic direction. While traditional motion editing can do small modifications, it cannot accommodate larger structural edits, resulting in visual warping artifacts that require authoring new motion. Conversely, recent advances in large-scale generative modeling have unlocked newfound capabilities for authoring entire movements by directly manipulating sparse spatial constraints. While impressive at creating new movements, these methods lack the capability to preserve and edit existing motion interactively. In this work, we introduce scheduled inpainting, a method that enables interactive generative motion editing, a novel paradigm unifying motion synthesis and editing by leveraging generative models. Scheduled inpainting is a simple yet powerful inference-based technique that enables fine-grained spatiotemporal control over the balance between preserving the original motion and generating new content. By building atop generative models that support direct manipulation, our system allows artists to interactively refine existing animations while ensuring results remain natural and consistent with the learned motion distribution. Scheduled inpainting is versatile and supports many editing applications, such as extending, stitching, and compositing different clips. Finally, we extensively validate our approach by comparing with four baselines, conducting ablations of our design, and reporting user feedback.

</details>
